What in the traditional text is one hymn, consists really  of four independent hymns of three verses each. This is to  be concluded from the well-known laws of arrangement of  the Samhita, and is confirmed by the change of metre  and by the reception of two of the four hymns into other  Vedic Samhitas: the second (verses 4-6) is found ...
